        Idea:
Implement undo/redo/history system for tools/video-editor

## Context
The video editor uses React Context + custom hooks. All mutations flow through applyTimelineEdit() in useTimelineSave.ts. There is no existing undo/redo.

## Requirements

### Two-tier history system:

**Tier 1: Undo Stack (fine-grained, in-memory)**
- Push snapshot of { rows, meta, clipOrder, tracks, output } before every applyTimelineEdit() call
- Cap at ~50-100 entries
- Cmd+Z / Cmd+Shift+Z
- Drag operations and rapid edits should collapse into single undo entries (batch by user gesture using transaction ID or debounce window)

**Tier 2: Checkpoints (coarse-grained, persisted to Supabase)**
- Named snapshots of full TimelineConfig stored in Supabase DB
- Keep checkpoints for past 24 hours
- Browsable - jump to any checkpoint
- Activity-aware checkpoint triggers:
  1. Session boundary: When edit arrives after >5min idle gap, checkpoint pre-edit state
  2. Edit-distance cap: After ~30 edits since last checkpoint, auto-checkpoint
  3. Semantic triggers: Before destructive ops (delete track, bulk delete, clear timeline) + manual Save checkpoint
- Checkpoint metadata: id, timestamp, trigger type, auto-generated label, editsSinceLastCheckpoint, config

### UI
- Undo/redo buttons + history dropdown placed next to the Saving... indicator in the editor chrome
- History panel shows checkpoint list with labels and trigger types

### Persistence
- Checkpoints stored in Supabase (JSONB column or separate table) for past 24 hours
- Undo stack is in-memory only (session-level)

### Implementation approach
- New useTimelineHistory hook that wraps/intercepts applyTimelineEdit
- Exposes: undo(), redo(), jumpToCheckpoint(id), createCheckpoint(label)
- Manages idle timer via ref tracking lastEditTimestamp
- Checkpoints cap at ~20-30, undo stack at ~50-100

    "SNAPSHOT CORRECTNESS: Snapshots must capture `currentData.config` (the last committed config), NOT `dataRef.current.rows/tracks`. Drop handlers (useAssetManagement.ts:164-165, useExternalDrop.ts:171-172, 197-202) mutate dataRef in-place before commitData \u2014 config is unaffected because it's rebuilt inside materializeData().",
    "AGENT CHAT PATH (DECISION-004): Agent edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling (useAgentSession.ts:288-295, useTimelineRealtime.ts:27-47), NOT through applyResolvedConfigEdit. These correctly bypass undo (polling commits use skipHistory). Do NOT attempt to make agent edits undoable \u2014 treat them like external/collaborative edits.",
    "DEBT: agent-chat-undo-integration \u2014 agent edits bypass undo stack by design. Do not make this worse by accidentally removing skipHistory from polling paths.",
    "DEBT: conflict-resolution \u2014 silently reloading agent updates can discard dirty local edits. The checkpoint system gives users a recovery path but doesn't fix the root issue. Don't introduce new silent-reload paths.",
    "PRE-MUTATION PROBLEM: Verify asset drop undo works correctly. The snapshot stores config from before the drop; on undo, buildDataFromCurrentRegistry rebuilds from that config, which should remove both the clip AND any auto-created track.",
    "TRANSACTION COLLAPSING: Two mechanisms \u2014 exact transactionId match (drag, resize, multi-clip move) and 300ms time window (untransacted rapid edits like sliders). These are intentionally separate. Don't unify them.",
    "CHECKPOINT EDIT COUNTING (minor flag): Transaction-collapsed gestures still count once per low-level commit toward the 30-edit auto-checkpoint cap. Acceptable for v1 \u2014 a drag of 20 intermediate positions counts as ~20 edits toward checkpoint threshold, not 1. Could refine later.",
    "SKIP_HISTORY COMPLETENESS: reloadFromServer (~line 436) and polled-data sync effect (~line 367) in useTimelineSave.ts must have skipHistory:true. If any new commit path is added during implementation, evaluate whether it should skip history.",
    "buildDataFromCurrentRegistry DEPENDENCY: Confirm this function exists in timeline-save-utils.ts and correctly rebuilds TimelineData from a config + current registry. It must preserve resolved URLs and registry entries.",
    "RLS POLICY: timeline_checkpoints must filter by user_id = auth.uid(). Users should never see other users' checkpoints even if they share a timeline_id.",
    "UNDO STACK SIZE: 100 entries \u00d7 ~10-20KB config = ~1-2MB. Monitor in practice. If configs grow larger (many clips), consider structural sharing or reducing cap.",
    "canUndo/canRedo STATE TRANSITIONS: Only update React state on empty\u2194non-empty transitions to avoid re-rendering the entire editor tree on every edit. Do not use derived state from stack length."

  "meta_commentary": "**Execution guidance:**\n\n**Critical path:** T1+T2 (parallel) \u2192 T3+T4 (parallel, T3 needs T1+T2, T4 needs T1) \u2192 T5 (needs T3+T4) \u2192 T6 (needs T5) \u2192 T7+T8+T9+T10+T11+T12 (parallel, various deps). T13 is the integration gate. T14 is deploy.\n\n**The single hardest thing:** T5 (useTimelineHistory) is the bulk of the work. Read `useTimelineSave.ts` thoroughly first \u2014 understand `commitData`, `materializeData`, `applyTimelineEdit`, and `applyResolvedConfigEdit` call flow. The snapshot strategy (config-based, not dataRef-based) is the key design decision. Get this right and everything else follows.\n\n**Pre-mutation problem (most subtle bug risk):** Drop handlers in useAssetManagement and useExternalDrop mutate `dataRef.current.tracks` and `dataRef.current.rows` IN PLACE before calling applyTimelineEdit. The onBeforeCommit callback receives `dataRef.current` which already has these mutations. BUT we only read `.config` and `.signature` from it \u2014 config is rebuilt by `materializeData()` inside `applyTimelineEdit`, so it still reflects the PREVIOUS committed state. This is why we snapshot config, not rows/tracks.\n\n**Agent chat edits (corrected assumption):** The plan originally claimed agent edits participate in undo via applyResolvedConfigEdit. This is WRONG \u2014 agent edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime (useAgentSession.ts:288-295), which triggers polling, which commits with skipHistory. This is actually correct behavior: externally-originated edits should not be undoable. Do not attempt to wire agent edits into the undo system.\n\n**Checkpoint edit counting (known minor imprecision):** During a drag, each intermediate position counts as 1 edit toward the 30-edit checkpoint cap, even though transaction collapsing means they're 1 undo entry. This is acceptable for v1 \u2014 it means checkpoints trigger slightly more often during heavy drag sessions, which is harmless.\n\n**buildDataFromCurrentRegistry:** Verify this function exists in timeline-save-utils.ts before writing T5. It's referenced in the plan but needs confirmation. It should take a TimelineConfig + current TimelineData and rebuild rows/meta/clipOrder/tracks/output from the config while preserving the registry and resolved URLs.\n\n**Deploy order:** Do NOT deploy the migration (T14) until all code changes pass tests (T13).         Idea:
Implement undo/redo/history system for tools/video-editor

## Context
The video editor uses React Context + custom hooks. All mutations flow through applyTimelineEdit() in useTimelineSave.ts. There is no existing undo/redo.

## Requirements

### Two-tier history system:

**Tier 1: Undo Stack (fine-grained, in-memory)**
- Push snapshot of { rows, meta, clipOrder, tracks, output } before every applyTimelineEdit() call
- Cap at ~50-100 entries
- Cmd+Z / Cmd+Shift+Z
- Drag operations and rapid edits should collapse into single undo entries (batch by user gesture using transaction ID or debounce window)

**Tier 2: Checkpoints (coarse-grained, persisted to Supabase)**
- Named snapshots of full TimelineConfig stored in Supabase DB
- Keep checkpoints for past 24 hours
- Browsable - jump to any checkpoint
- Activity-aware checkpoint triggers:
  1. Session boundary: When edit arrives after >5min idle gap, checkpoint pre-edit state
  2. Edit-distance cap: After ~30 edits since last checkpoint, auto-checkpoint
  3. Semantic triggers: Before destructive ops (delete track, bulk delete, clear timeline) + manual Save checkpoint
- Checkpoint metadata: id, timestamp, trigger type, auto-generated label, editsSinceLastCheckpoint, config

### UI
- Undo/redo buttons + history dropdown placed next to the Saving... indicator in the editor chrome
- History panel shows checkpoint list with labels and trigger types

### Persistence
- Checkpoints stored in Supabase (JSONB column or separate table) for past 24 hours
- Undo stack is in-memory only (session-level)

### Implementation approach
- New useTimelineHistory hook that wraps/intercepts applyTimelineEdit
- Exposes: undo(), redo(), jumpToCheckpoint(id), createCheckpoint(label)
- Manages idle timer via ref tracking lastEditTimestamp
- Checkpoints cap at ~20-30, undo stack at ~50-100

  "watch_items": [
    "SNAPSHOT CORRECTNESS: Snapshots must capture `currentData.config` (the last committed config), NOT `dataRef.current.rows/tracks`. Drop handlers (useAssetManagement.ts:164-165, useExternalDrop.ts:171-172, 197-202) mutate dataRef in-place before commitData \u2014 config is unaffected because it's rebuilt inside materializeData().",
    "AGENT CHAT PATH (DECISION-004): Agent edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling (useAgentSession.ts:288-295, useTimelineRealtime.ts:27-47), NOT through applyResolvedConfigEdit. These correctly bypass undo (polling commits use skipHistory). Do NOT attempt to make agent edits undoable \u2014 treat them like external/collaborative edits.",
    "DEBT: agent-chat-undo-integration \u2014 agent edits bypass undo stack by design. Do not make this worse by accidentally removing skipHistory from polling paths.",
    "DEBT: conflict-resolution \u2014 silently reloading agent updates can discard dirty local edits. The checkpoint system gives users a recovery path but doesn't fix the root issue. Don't introduce new silent-reload paths.",
    "PRE-MUTATION PROBLEM: Verify asset drop undo works correctly. The snapshot stores config from before the drop; on undo, buildDataFromCurrentRegistry rebuilds from that config, which should remove both the clip AND any auto-created track.",
    "TRANSACTION COLLAPSING: Two mechanisms \u2014 exact transactionId match (drag, resize, multi-clip move) and 300ms time window (untransacted rapid edits like sliders). These are intentionally separate. Don't unify them.",
    "CHECKPOINT EDIT COUNTING (minor flag): Transaction-collapsed gestures still count once per low-level commit toward the 30-edit auto-checkpoint cap. Acceptable for v1 \u2014 a drag of 20 intermediate positions counts as ~20 edits toward checkpoint threshold, not 1. Could refine later.",
    "SKIP_HISTORY COMPLETENESS: reloadFromServer (~line 436) and polled-data sync effect (~line 367) in useTimelineSave.ts must have skipHistory:true. If any new commit path is added during implementation, evaluate whether it should skip history.",
    "buildDataFromCurrentRegistry DEPENDENCY: Confirm this function exists in timeline-save-utils.ts and correctly rebuilds TimelineData from a config + current registry. It must preserve resolved URLs and registry entries.",
    "RLS POLICY: timeline_checkpoints must filter by user_id = auth.uid(). Users should never see other users' checkpoints even if they share a timeline_id.",
    "UNDO STACK SIZE: 100 entries \u00d7 ~10-20KB config = ~1-2MB. Monitor in practice. If configs grow larger (many clips), consider structural sharing or reducing cap.",
    "canUndo/canRedo STATE TRANSITIONS: Only update React state on empty\u2194non-empty transitions to avoid re-rendering the entire editor tree on every edit. Do not use derived state from stack length."
  ],

  "meta_commentary": "**Execution guidance:**\n\n**Critical path:** T1+T2 (parallel) \u2192 T3+T4 (parallel, T3 needs T1+T2, T4 needs T1) \u2192 T5 (needs T3+T4) \u2192 T6 (needs T5) \u2192 T7+T8+T9+T10+T11+T12 (parallel, various deps). T13 is the integration gate. T14 is deploy.\n\n**The single hardest thing:** T5 (useTimelineHistory) is the bulk of the work. Read `useTimelineSave.ts` thoroughly first \u2014 understand `commitData`, `materializeData`, `applyTimelineEdit`, and `applyResolvedConfigEdit` call flow. The snapshot strategy (config-based, not dataRef-based) is the key design decision. Get this right and everything else follows.\n\n**Pre-mutation problem (most subtle bug risk):** Drop handlers in useAssetManagement and useExternalDrop mutate `dataRef.current.tracks` and `dataRef.current.rows` IN PLACE before calling applyTimelineEdit. The onBeforeCommit callback receives `dataRef.current` which already has these mutations. BUT we only read `.config` and `.signature` from it \u2014 config is rebuilt by `materializeData()` inside `applyTimelineEdit`, so it still reflects the PREVIOUS committed state. This is why we snapshot config, not rows/tracks.\n\n**Agent chat edits (corrected assumption):** The plan originally claimed agent edits participate in undo via applyResolvedConfigEdit. This is WRONG \u2014 agent edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime (useAgentSession.ts:288-295), which triggers polling, which commits with skipHistory. This is actually correct behavior: externally-originated edits should not be undoable. Do not attempt to wire agent edits into the undo system.\n\n**Checkpoint edit counting (known minor imprecision):** During a drag, each intermediate position counts as 1 edit toward the 30-edit checkpoint cap, even though transaction collapsing means they're 1 undo entry. This is acceptable for v1 \u2014 it means checkpoints trigger slightly more often during heavy drag sessions, which is harmless.\n\n**buildDataFromCurrentRegistry:** Verify this function exists in timeline-save-utils.ts before writing T5. It's referenced in the plan but needs confirmation. It should take a TimelineConfig + current TimelineData and rebuild rows/meta/clipOrder/tracks/output from the config while preserving the registry and resolved URLs.\n\n**Deploy order:** Do NOT deploy the migration (T14) until all code changes pass tests (T13).         Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-panel-tabs: text clip multi-selections would miss the position tab under the plan's stated rule. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-properties-panel: bulkclippanel still imports customeffecteditor; removing it without updating bulk mode would break the build. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] clip-drag-scroll-sync: the plan's step 3 ontick callback only shows coordinator.update() but the full clip-drag state path (snap, lateststart, ghost positions) also needs re-running during stationary-pointer scroll. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: silently reloading agent-originated timeline updates would discard dirty local edits. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] context-menu-scope: multi-clip copy is not included despite being mentioned in the original idea. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] issue-detection: mid-sentence-cut detection is not implementable without transcript/profile data that the current provider doesn't expose. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] legacy-effect-lifecycle: removing customeffecteditor makes effects-table entries read-only — users can no longer edit legacy custom effects. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] optimistic-locking: plan only specifies version checking at the db write layer; the ui load/state/save path doesn't carry config_version yet. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] properties-panel-scope: crop and transition controls are not included in the bulk panel, narrowing the original idea's scope. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] selection-save-bridge: the plan specifies passing pruneselection into usetimelinesave but doesn't detail the hook-layer bridge needed because usetimelinesave is instantiated inside usetimelinedata before usetimelinestate runs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] tool-error-propagation: a blanket catch in executetool would silently downgrade db/persistence failures into ordinary tool results, changing the session error/http 500 behavior. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-accessibility: replacing arrow buttons with drag-only handle removes keyboard-accessible reorder; plan only specifies pointersensor. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-testing: no automated tests specified for same-kind boundary rules or save/load persistence of reordered tracks. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Idea:
Implement undo/redo/history system for tools/video-editor

## Context
The video editor uses React Context + custom hooks. All mutations flow through applyTimelineEdit() in useTimelineSave.ts. There is no existing undo/redo.

## Requirements

### Two-tier history system:

**Tier 1: Undo Stack (fine-grained, in-memory)**
- Push snapshot of { rows, meta, clipOrder, tracks, output } before every applyTimelineEdit() call
- Cap at ~50-100 entries
- Cmd+Z / Cmd+Shift+Z
- Drag operations and rapid edits should collapse into single undo entries (batch by user gesture using transaction ID or debounce window)

**Tier 2: Checkpoints (coarse-grained, persisted to Supabase)**
- Named snapshots of full TimelineConfig stored in Supabase DB
- Keep checkpoints for past 24 hours
- Browsable - jump to any checkpoint
- Activity-aware checkpoint triggers:
  1. Session boundary: When edit arrives after >5min idle gap, checkpoint pre-edit state
  2. Edit-distance cap: After ~30 edits since last checkpoint, auto-checkpoint
  3. Semantic triggers: Before destructive ops (delete track, bulk delete, clear timeline) + manual Save checkpoint
- Checkpoint metadata: id, timestamp, trigger type, auto-generated label, editsSinceLastCheckpoint, config

### UI
- Undo/redo buttons + history dropdown placed next to the Saving... indicator in the editor chrome
- History panel shows checkpoint list with labels and trigger types

### Persistence
- Checkpoints stored in Supabase (JSONB column or separate table) for past 24 hours
- Undo stack is in-memory only (session-level)

### Implementation approach
- New useTimelineHistory hook that wraps/intercepts applyTimelineEdit
- Exposes: undo(), redo(), jumpToCheckpoint(id), createCheckpoint(label)
- Manages idle timer via ref tracking lastEditTimestamp
- Checkpoints cap at ~20-30, undo stack at ~50-100

    "description": "Create `useTimelineHistory` hook at `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineHistory.ts`. (2) `onBeforeCommit` callback snapshots `currentData.config` (NOT dataRef \u2014 avoids pre-mutation problem). (3) Transaction collapsing: skip push if transactionId matches top entry's; 300ms debounce for untransacted rapid edits.   "watch_items": [
    "SNAPSHOT CORRECTNESS: Snapshots must capture `currentData.config` (the last committed config), NOT `dataRef.current.rows/tracks`. Drop handlers (useAssetManagement.ts:164-165, useExternalDrop.ts:171-172, 197-202) mutate dataRef in-place before commitData \u2014 config is unaffected because it's rebuilt inside materializeData().",
    "AGENT CHAT PATH (DECISION-004): Agent edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling (useAgentSession.ts:288-295, useTimelineRealtime.ts:27-47), NOT through applyResolvedConfigEdit. These correctly bypass undo (polling commits use skipHistory). Do NOT attempt to make agent edits undoable \u2014 treat them like external/collaborative edits.",
    "DEBT: agent-chat-undo-integration \u2014 agent edits bypass undo stack by design. Do not make this worse by accidentally removing skipHistory from polling paths.",
    "DEBT: conflict-resolution \u2014 silently reloading agent updates can discard dirty local edits. The checkpoint system gives users a recovery path but doesn't fix the root issue. Don't introduce new silent-reload paths.",
    "PRE-MUTATION PROBLEM: Verify asset drop undo works correctly. The snapshot stores config from before the drop; on undo, buildDataFromCurrentRegistry rebuilds from that config, which should remove both the clip AND any auto-created track.",
    "TRANSACTION COLLAPSING: Two mechanisms \u2014 exact transactionId match (drag, resize, multi-clip move) and 300ms time window (untransacted rapid edits like sliders). These are intentionally separate. Don't unify them.",
    "CHECKPOINT EDIT COUNTING (minor flag): Transaction-collapsed gestures still count once per low-level commit toward the 30-edit auto-checkpoint cap. Acceptable for v1 \u2014 a drag of 20 intermediate positions counts as ~20 edits toward checkpoint threshold, not 1. Could refine later.",
    "SKIP_HISTORY COMPLETENESS: reloadFromServer (~line 436) and polled-data sync effect (~line 367) in useTimelineSave.ts must have skipHistory:true. If any new commit path is added during implementation, evaluate whether it should skip history.",
    "buildDataFromCurrentRegistry DEPENDENCY: Confirm this function exists in timeline-save-utils.ts and correctly rebuilds TimelineData from a config + current registry. It must preserve resolved URLs and registry entries.",
    "RLS POLICY: timeline_checkpoints must filter by user_id = auth.uid(). Users should never see other users' checkpoints even if they share a timeline_id.",
    "UNDO STACK SIZE: 100 entries \u00d7 ~10-20KB config = ~1-2MB. Monitor in practice. If configs grow larger (many clips), consider structural sharing or reducing cap.",
    "canUndo/canRedo STATE TRANSITIONS: Only update React state on empty\u2194non-empty transitions to avoid re-rendering the entire editor tree on every edit. Do not use derived state from stack length."
  ],

  "meta_commentary": "**Execution guidance:**\n\n**Critical path:** T1+T2 (parallel) \u2192 T3+T4 (parallel, T3 needs T1+T2, T4 needs T1) \u2192 T5 (needs T3+T4) \u2192 T6 (needs T5) \u2192 T7+T8+T9+T10+T11+T12 (parallel, various deps). T13 is the integration gate. T14 is deploy.\n\n**The single hardest thing:** T5 (useTimelineHistory) is the bulk of the work. Read `useTimelineSave.ts` thoroughly first \u2014 understand `commitData`, `materializeData`, `applyTimelineEdit`, and `applyResolvedConfigEdit` call flow. The snapshot strategy (config-based, not dataRef-based) is the key design decision. Get this right and everything else follows.\n\n**Pre-mutation problem (most subtle bug risk):** Drop handlers in useAssetManagement and useExternalDrop mutate `dataRef.current.tracks` and `dataRef.current.rows` IN PLACE before calling applyTimelineEdit. The onBeforeCommit callback receives `dataRef.current` which already has these mutations. BUT we only read `.config` and `.signature` from it \u2014 config is rebuilt by `materializeData()` inside `applyTimelineEdit`, so it still reflects the PREVIOUS committed state. This is why we snapshot config, not rows/tracks.\n\n**Agent chat edits (corrected assumption):** The plan originally claimed agent edits participate in undo via applyResolvedConfigEdit. This is WRONG \u2014 agent edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime (useAgentSession.ts:288-295), which triggers polling, which commits with skipHistory. This is actually correct behavior: externally-originated edits should not be undoable. Do not attempt to wire agent edits into the undo system.\n\n**Checkpoint edit counting (known minor imprecision):** During a drag, each intermediate position counts as 1 edit toward the 30-edit checkpoint cap, even though transaction collapsing means they're 1 undo entry. This is acceptable for v1 \u2014 it means checkpoints trigger slightly more often during heavy drag sessions, which is harmless.\n\n**buildDataFromCurrentRegistry:** Verify this function exists in timeline-save-utils.ts before writing T5. It's referenced in the plan but needs confirmation. It should take a TimelineConfig + current TimelineData and rebuild rows/meta/clipOrder/tracks/output from the config while preserving the registry and resolved URLs.\n\n**Deploy order:** Do NOT deploy the migration (T14) until all code changes pass tests (T13). The migration creates the table but existing code won't break without it \u2014 the DataProvider methods are optional.\n\n**Supabase safety:** Deploy with `npx supabase db push --linked` (never `db reset --linked`). Deploy functions individually if any edge functions are touched (none in this plan)."
}

        Idea:
Implement undo/redo/history system for tools/video-editor

## Context
The video editor uses React Context + custom hooks. All mutations flow through applyTimelineEdit() in useTimelineSave.ts. There is no existing undo/redo.

## Requirements

### Two-tier history system:

**Tier 1: Undo Stack (fine-grained, in-memory)**
- Push snapshot of { rows, meta, clipOrder, tracks, output } before every applyTimelineEdit() call
- Cap at ~50-100 entries
- Cmd+Z / Cmd+Shift+Z
- Drag operations and rapid edits should collapse into single undo entries (batch by user gesture using transaction ID or debounce window)

**Tier 2: Checkpoints (coarse-grained, persisted to Supabase)**
- Named snapshots of full TimelineConfig stored in Supabase DB
- Keep checkpoints for past 24 hours
- Browsable - jump to any checkpoint
- Activity-aware checkpoint triggers:
  1. Session boundary: When edit arrives after >5min idle gap, checkpoint pre-edit state
  2. Edit-distance cap: After ~30 edits since last checkpoint, auto-checkpoint
  3. Semantic triggers: Before destructive ops (delete track, bulk delete, clear timeline) + manual Save checkpoint
- Checkpoint metadata: id, timestamp, trigger type, auto-generated label, editsSinceLastCheckpoint, config

### UI
- Undo/redo buttons + history dropdown placed next to the Saving... indicator in the editor chrome
- History panel shows checkpoint list with labels and trigger types

### Persistence
- Checkpoints stored in Supabase (JSONB column or separate table) for past 24 hours
- Undo stack is in-memory only (session-level)

### Implementation approach
- New useTimelineHistory hook that wraps/intercepts applyTimelineEdit
- Exposes: undo(), redo(), jumpToCheckpoint(id), createCheckpoint(label)
- Manages idle timer via ref tracking lastEditTimestamp
- Checkpoints cap at ~20-30, undo stack at ~50-100

  "watch_items": [
    "SNAPSHOT CORRECTNESS: Snapshots must capture `currentData.config` (the last committed config), NOT `dataRef.current.rows/tracks`. Drop handlers (useAssetManagement.ts:164-165, useExternalDrop.ts:171-172, 197-202) mutate dataRef in-place before commitData \u2014 config is unaffected because it's rebuilt inside materializeData().",
    "AGENT CHAT PATH (DECISION-004): Agent edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling (useAgentSession.ts:288-295, useTimelineRealtime.ts:27-47), NOT through applyResolvedConfigEdit. These correctly bypass undo (polling commits use skipHistory). Do NOT attempt to make agent edits undoable \u2014 treat them like external/collaborative edits.",
    "DEBT: agent-chat-undo-integration \u2014 agent edits bypass undo stack by design. Do not make this worse by accidentally removing skipHistory from polling paths.",
    "DEBT: conflict-resolution \u2014 silently reloading agent updates can discard dirty local edits. The checkpoint system gives users a recovery path but doesn't fix the root issue. Don't introduce new silent-reload paths.",
    "PRE-MUTATION PROBLEM: Verify asset drop undo works correctly. The snapshot stores config from before the drop; on undo, buildDataFromCurrentRegistry rebuilds from that config, which should remove both the clip AND any auto-created track.",
    "TRANSACTION COLLAPSING: Two mechanisms \u2014 exact transactionId match (drag, resize, multi-clip move) and 300ms time window (untransacted rapid edits like sliders). These are intentionally separate. Don't unify them.",
    "CHECKPOINT EDIT COUNTING (minor flag): Transaction-collapsed gestures still count once per low-level commit toward the 30-edit auto-checkpoint cap. Acceptable for v1 \u2014 a drag of 20 intermediate positions counts as ~20 edits toward checkpoint threshold, not 1. Could refine later.",
    "SKIP_HISTORY COMPLETENESS: reloadFromServer (~line 436) and polled-data sync effect (~line 367) in useTimelineSave.ts must have skipHistory:true. If any new commit path is added during implementation, evaluate whether it should skip history.",
    "buildDataFromCurrentRegistry DEPENDENCY: Confirm this function exists in timeline-save-utils.ts and correctly rebuilds TimelineData from a config + current registry. It must preserve resolved URLs and registry entries.",
    "RLS POLICY: timeline_checkpoints must filter by user_id = auth.uid(). Users should never see other users' checkpoints even if they share a timeline_id.",
    "UNDO STACK SIZE: 100 entries \u00d7 ~10-20KB config = ~1-2MB. Monitor in practice. If configs grow larger (many clips), consider structural sharing or reducing cap.",
    "canUndo/canRedo STATE TRANSITIONS: Only update React state on empty\u2194non-empty transitions to avoid re-rendering the entire editor tree on every edit. Do not use derived state from stack length."
  ],

  "meta_commentary": "**Execution guidance:**\n\n**Critical path:** T1+T2 (parallel) \u2192 T3+T4 (parallel, T3 needs T1+T2, T4 needs T1) \u2192 T5 (needs T3+T4) \u2192 T6 (needs T5) \u2192 T7+T8+T9+T10+T11+T12 (parallel, various deps). T13 is the integration gate. T14 is deploy.\n\n**The single hardest thing:** T5 (useTimelineHistory) is the bulk of the work. Read `useTimelineSave.ts` thoroughly first \u2014 understand `commitData`, `materializeData`, `applyTimelineEdit`, and `applyResolvedConfigEdit` call flow. The snapshot strategy (config-based, not dataRef-based) is the key design decision. Get this right and everything else follows.\n\n**Pre-mutation problem (most subtle bug risk):** Drop handlers in useAssetManagement and useExternalDrop mutate `dataRef.current.tracks` and `dataRef.current.rows` IN PLACE before calling applyTimelineEdit. The onBeforeCommit callback receives `dataRef.current` which already has these mutations. BUT we only read `.config` and `.signature` from it \u2014 config is rebuilt by `materializeData()` inside `applyTimelineEdit`, so it still reflects the PREVIOUS committed state. This is why we snapshot config, not rows/tracks.\n\n**Agent chat edits (corrected assumption):** The plan originally claimed agent edits participate in undo via applyResolvedConfigEdit. This is WRONG \u2014 agent edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime (useAgentSession.ts:288-295), which triggers polling, which commits with skipHistory. This is actually correct behavior: externally-originated edits should not be undoable. Do not attempt to wire agent edits into the undo system.\n\n**Checkpoint edit counting (known minor imprecision):** During a drag, each intermediate position counts as 1 edit toward the 30-edit checkpoint cap, even though transaction collapsing means they're 1 undo entry. This is acceptable for v1 \u2014 it means checkpoints trigger slightly more often during heavy drag sessions, which is harmless.\n\n**buildDataFromCurrentRegistry:** Verify this function exists in timeline-save-utils.ts before writing T5. It's referenced in the plan but needs confirmation. It should take a TimelineConfig + current TimelineData and rebuild rows/meta/clipOrder/tracks/output from the config while preserving the registry and resolved URLs.\n\n**Deploy order:** Do NOT deploy the migration (T14) until all code changes pass tests (T13). The migration creates the table but existing code won't break without it \u2014 the DataProvider methods are optional.\n\n**Supabase safety:** Deploy with `npx supabase db push --linked` (never `db reset --linked`). Deploy functions individually if any edge functions are touched (none in this plan)."
}

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Implement undo/redo/history system for tools/video-editor

## Context
The video editor uses React Context + custom hooks. All mutations flow through applyTimelineEdit() in useTimelineSave.ts. There is no existing undo/redo.

## Requirements

### Two-tier history system:

**Tier 1: Undo Stack (fine-grained, in-memory)**
- Push snapshot of { rows, meta, clipOrder, tracks, output } before every applyTimelineEdit() call
- Cap at ~50-100 entries
- Cmd+Z / Cmd+Shift+Z
- Drag operations and rapid edits should collapse into single undo entries (batch by user gesture using transaction ID or debounce window)

**Tier 2: Checkpoints (coarse-grained, persisted to Supabase)**
- Named snapshots of full TimelineConfig stored in Supabase DB
- Keep checkpoints for past 24 hours
- Browsable - jump to any checkpoint
- Activity-aware checkpoint triggers:
  1. Session boundary: When edit arrives after >5min idle gap, checkpoint pre-edit state
  2. Edit-distance cap: After ~30 edits since last checkpoint, auto-checkpoint
  3. Semantic triggers: Before destructive ops (delete track, bulk delete, clear timeline) + manual Save checkpoint
- Checkpoint metadata: id, timestamp, trigger type, auto-generated label, editsSinceLastCheckpoint, config

### UI
- Undo/redo buttons + history dropdown placed next to the Saving... indicator in the editor chrome
- History panel shows checkpoint list with labels and trigger types

### Persistence
- Checkpoints stored in Supabase (JSONB column or separate table) for past 24 hours
- Undo stack is in-memory only (session-level)

### Implementation approach
- New useTimelineHistory hook that wraps/intercepts applyTimelineEdit
- Exposes: undo(), redo(), jumpToCheckpoint(id), createCheckpoint(label)
- Manages idle timer via ref tracking lastEditTimestamp
- Checkpoints cap at ~20-30, undo stack at ~50-100

(2) `onBeforeCommit` callback snapshots `currentData.config` (NOT dataRef \u2014 avoids pre-mutation problem). (3) Transaction collapsing: skip push if transactionId matches top entry's; 300ms debounce for untransacted rapid edits. (4) Undo: pop from undo stack, push current config to redo, rebuild TimelineData via `buildDataFromCurrentRegistry(snapshot.config, dataRef.current)`, call `commitData(rebuilt, {save:true, skipHistory:true})`. Redo mirrors. (5) Checkpoint triggers inside onBeforeCommit: session boundary (>5min idle), edit-distance (30 edits), semantic flag. Fire-and-forget `provider.saveCheckpoint()`. (6) Load checkpoints on mount. (7) `jumpToCheckpoint`: restore config, clear both stacks. (8) `createManualCheckpoint`: save current config immediately. Cap undo at 100 entries.",

  "meta_commentary": "**Execution guidance:**\n\n**Critical path:** T1+T2 (parallel) \u2192 T3+T4 (parallel, T3 needs T1+T2, T4 needs T1) \u2192 T5 (needs T3+T4) \u2192 T6 (needs T5) \u2192 T7+T8+T9+T10+T11+T12 (parallel, various deps). T13 is the integration gate. T14 is deploy.\n\n**The single hardest thing:** T5 (useTimelineHistory) is the bulk of the work. Read `useTimelineSave.ts` thoroughly first \u2014 understand `commitData`, `materializeData`, `applyTimelineEdit`, and `applyResolvedConfigEdit` call flow. The snapshot strategy (config-based, not dataRef-based) is the key design decision. Get this right and everything else follows.\n\n**Pre-mutation problem (most subtle bug risk):** Drop handlers in useAssetManagement and useExternalDrop mutate `dataRef.current.tracks` and `dataRef.current.rows` IN PLACE before calling applyTimelineEdit. The onBeforeCommit callback receives `dataRef.current` which already has these mutations. BUT we only read `.config` and `.signature` from it \u2014 config is rebuilt by `materializeData()` inside `applyTimelineEdit`, so it still reflects the PREVIOUS committed state. This is why we snapshot config, not rows/tracks.\n\n**Agent chat edits (corrected assumption):** The plan originally claimed agent edits participate in undo via applyResolvedConfigEdit. This is WRONG \u2014 agent edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime (useAgentSession.ts:288-295), which triggers polling, which commits with skipHistory. This is actually correct behavior: externally-originated edits should not be undoable. Do not attempt to wire agent edits into the undo system.\n\n**Checkpoint edit counting (known minor imprecision):** During a drag, each intermediate position counts as 1 edit toward the 30-edit checkpoint cap, even though transaction collapsing means they're 1 undo entry. This is acceptable for v1 \u2014 it means checkpoints trigger slightly more often during heavy drag sessions, which is harmless.\n\n**buildDataFromCurrentRegistry:** Verify this function exists in timeline-save-utils.ts before writing T5. It's referenced in the plan but needs confirmation. It should take a TimelineConfig + current TimelineData and rebuild rows/meta/clipOrder/tracks/output from the config while preserving the registry and resolved URLs.\n\n**Deploy order:** Do NOT deploy the migration (T14) until all code changes pass tests (T13). The migration creates the table but existing code won't break without it \u2014 the DataProvider methods are optional.\n\n**Supabase safety:** Deploy with `npx supabase db push --linked` (never `db reset --linked`). Deploy functions individually if any edge functions are touched (none in this plan)."
}

        Idea:
Implement undo/redo/history system for tools/video-editor

## Context
The video editor uses React Context + custom hooks. All mutations flow through applyTimelineEdit() in useTimelineSave.ts. There is no existing undo/redo.

## Requirements

### Two-tier history system:

**Tier 1: Undo Stack (fine-grained, in-memory)**
- Push snapshot of { rows, meta, clipOrder, tracks, output } before every applyTimelineEdit() call
- Cap at ~50-100 entries
- Cmd+Z / Cmd+Shift+Z
- Drag operations and rapid edits should collapse into single undo entries (batch by user gesture using transaction ID or debounce window)

**Tier 2: Checkpoints (coarse-grained, persisted to Supabase)**
- Named snapshots of full TimelineConfig stored in Supabase DB
- Keep checkpoints for past 24 hours
- Browsable - jump to any checkpoint
- Activity-aware checkpoint triggers:
  1. Session boundary: When edit arrives after >5min idle gap, checkpoint pre-edit state
  2. Edit-distance cap: After ~30 edits since last checkpoint, auto-checkpoint
  3. Semantic triggers: Before destructive ops (delete track, bulk delete, clear timeline) + manual Save checkpoint
- Checkpoint metadata: id, timestamp, trigger type, auto-generated label, editsSinceLastCheckpoint, config

### UI
- Undo/redo buttons + history dropdown placed next to the Saving... indicator in the editor chrome
- History panel shows checkpoint list with labels and trigger types

### Persistence
- Checkpoints stored in Supabase (JSONB column or separate table) for past 24 hours
- Undo stack is in-memory only (session-level)

### Implementation approach
- New useTimelineHistory hook that wraps/intercepts applyTimelineEdit
- Exposes: undo(), redo(), jumpToCheckpoint(id), createCheckpoint(label)
- Manages idle timer via ref tracking lastEditTimestamp
- Checkpoints cap at ~20-30, undo stack at ~50-100

    "id": "T5",
    "description": "Create `useTimelineHistory` hook at `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineHistory.ts`. Core implementation: (1) Undo/redo stacks as refs, canUndo/canRedo as state updated on empty\u2194non-empty transitions. (2) `onBeforeCommit` callback snapshots `currentData.config` (NOT dataRef \u2014 avoids pre-mutation problem). (3) Transaction collapsing: skip push if transactionId matches top entry's; 300ms debounce for untransacted rapid edits. (4) Undo: pop from undo stack, push current config to redo, rebuild TimelineData via `buildDataFromCurrentRegistry(snapshot.config, dataRef.current)`, call `commitData(rebuilt, {save:true, skipHistory:true})`. Redo mirrors. (5) Checkpoint triggers inside onBeforeCommit: session boundary (>5min idle), edit-distance (30 edits), semantic flag. Fire-and-forget `provider.saveCheckpoint()`. (6) Load checkpoints on mount. (7) `jumpToCheckpoint`: restore config, clear both stacks. (8) `createManualCheckpoint`: save current config immediately. Cap undo at 100 entries.",
    "depends_on": [
      "T3",
      "T4"
    ],
    "status": "done",

  "meta_commentary": "**Execution guidance:**\n\n**Critical path:** T1+T2 (parallel) \u2192 T3+T4 (parallel, T3 needs T1+T2, T4 needs T1) \u2192 T5 (needs T3+T4) \u2192 T6 (needs T5) \u2192 T7+T8+T9+T10+T11+T12 (parallel, various deps). T13 is the integration gate. T14 is deploy.\n\n**The single hardest thing:** T5 (useTimelineHistory) is the bulk of the work. Read `useTimelineSave.ts` thoroughly first \u2014 understand `commitData`, `materializeData`, `applyTimelineEdit`, and `applyResolvedConfigEdit` call flow. The snapshot strategy (config-based, not dataRef-based) is the key design decision. Get this right and everything else follows.\n\n**Pre-mutation problem (most subtle bug risk):** Drop handlers in useAssetManagement and useExternalDrop mutate `dataRef.current.tracks` and `dataRef.current.rows` IN PLACE before calling applyTimelineEdit. The onBeforeCommit callback receives `dataRef.current` which already has these mutations. BUT we only read `.config` and `.signature` from it \u2014 config is rebuilt by `materializeData()` inside `applyTimelineEdit`, so it still reflects the PREVIOUS committed state. This is why we snapshot config, not rows/tracks.\n\n**Agent chat edits (corrected assumption):** The plan originally claimed agent edits participate in undo via applyResolvedConfigEdit. This is WRONG \u2014 agent edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime (useAgentSession.ts:288-295), which triggers polling, which commits with skipHistory. This is actually correct behavior: externally-originated edits should not be undoable. Do not attempt to wire agent edits into the undo system.\n\n**Checkpoint edit counting (known minor imprecision):** During a drag, each intermediate position counts as 1 edit toward the 30-edit checkpoint cap, even though transaction collapsing means they're 1 undo entry. This is acceptable for v1 \u2014 it means checkpoints trigger slightly more often during heavy drag sessions, which is harmless.\n\n**buildDataFromCurrentRegistry:** Verify this function exists in timeline-save-utils.ts before writing T5. It's referenced in the plan but needs confirmation. It should take a TimelineConfig + current TimelineData and rebuild rows/meta/clipOrder/tracks/output from the config while preserving the registry and resolved URLs.\n\n**Deploy order:** Do NOT deploy the migration (T14) until all code changes pass tests (T13). The migration creates the table but existing code won't break without it \u2014 the DataProvider methods are optional.\n\n**Supabase safety:** Deploy with `npx supabase db push --linked` (never `db reset --linked`). Deploy functions individually if any edge functions are touched (none in this plan)."
}

 >\n+        <Redo2 className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5\" />\n+      </Button>\n+      <DropdownMenu>\n+        <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>\n+          <Button type=\"button\" variant=\"ghost\" size=\"icon\" className=\"h-6 w-6\" title=\"History\">\n+            <History className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5\" />\n+          </Button>\n+        </DropdownMenuTrigger>\n+        <DropdownMenuContent align=\"start\" className=\"w-80\">\n+          <DropdownMenuLabel className=\"pb-1 text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground\">\n+            History\n+          </DropdownMenuLabel>\n+          <DropdownMenuSeparator />\n+          {chrome.checkpoints.length === 0 ? (\n+            <div className=\"px-2 py-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground\">\n+              No checkpoints yet. Save one manually or keep editing to build history.\n+            </div>\n+          ) : (\n+            chrome.checkpoints.map((checkpoint) => (\n+              <DropdownMenuItem\n+                key={checkpoint.id}\n+                className=\"flex flex-col items-start gap-1 py-2\"\n+                onClick={() => chrome.jumpToCheckpoint(checkpoint.id)}\n+              >\n+                <div className=\"flex w-full items-start justify-between gap-2\">\n+                  <span className=\"truncate text-sm text-foreground\">{checkpoint.label}</span>\n+                  <Badge\n+                    variant={CHECKPOINT_TRIGGER_BADGE_VARIANT[checkpoint.triggerType]}\n+                    className=\"shrink-0 px-1.5 py-0 text-[9px] uppercase tracking-[0.12em]\"\n+                  >\n+                    {CHECKPOINT_TRIGGER_LABELS[checkpoint.triggerType]}\n+                  </Badge>\n+                </div>\n+                <span className=\"text-[11px] text-muted-foreground\">\n+                  {formatDistanceToNow(new Date(checkpoint.createdAt), { addSuffix: true })}\n+                </span>\n+              </DropdownMenuItem>\n+            ))\n+          )}\n+         Idea:
Implement undo/redo/history system for tools/video-editor

## Context
The video editor uses React Context + custom hooks. All mutations flow through applyTimelineEdit() in useTimelineSave.ts. There is no existing undo/redo.

## Requirements

### Two-tier history system:

**Tier 1: Undo Stack (fine-grained, in-memory)**
- Push snapshot of { rows, meta, clipOrder, tracks, output } before every applyTimelineEdit() call
- Cap at ~50-100 entries
- Cmd+Z / Cmd+Shift+Z
- Drag operations and rapid edits should collapse into single undo entries (batch by user gesture using transaction ID or debounce window)

**Tier 2: Checkpoints (coarse-grained, persisted to Supabase)**
- Named snapshots of full TimelineConfig stored in Supabase DB
- Keep checkpoints for past 24 hours
- Browsable - jump to any checkpoint
- Activity-aware checkpoint triggers:
  1. Session boundary: When edit arrives after >5min idle gap, checkpoint pre-edit state
  2. Edit-distance cap: After ~30 edits since last checkpoint, auto-checkpoint
  3. Semantic triggers: Before destructive ops (delete track, bulk delete, clear timeline) + manual Save checkpoint
- Checkpoint metadata: id, timestamp, trigger type, auto-generated label, editsSinceLastCheckpoint, config

### UI
- Undo/redo buttons + history dropdown placed next to the Saving... indicator in the editor chrome
- History panel shows checkpoint list with labels and trigger types

### Persistence
- Checkpoints stored in Supabase (JSONB column or separate table) for past 24 hours
- Undo stack is in-memory only (session-level)

### Implementation approach
- New useTimelineHistory hook that wraps/intercepts applyTimelineEdit
- Exposes: undo(), redo(), jumpToCheckpoint(id), createCheckpoint(label)
- Manages idle timer via ref tracking lastEditTimestamp
- Checkpoints cap at ~20-30, undo stack at ~50-100

(3) Transaction collapsing: skip push if transactionId matches top entry's; 300ms debounce for untransacted rapid edits. (4) Undo: pop from undo stack, push current config to redo, rebuild TimelineData via `buildDataFromCurrentRegistry(snapshot.config, dataRef.current)`, call `commitData(rebuilt, {save:true, skipHistory:true})`. Redo mirrors. (5) Checkpoint triggers inside onBeforeCommit: session boundary (>5min idle), edit-distance (30 edits), semantic flag. Fire-and-forget `provider.saveCheckpoint()`.   "watch_items": [
    "SNAPSHOT CORRECTNESS: Snapshots must capture `currentData.config` (the last committed config), NOT `dataRef.current.rows/tracks`. Drop handlers (useAssetManagement.ts:164-165, useExternalDrop.ts:171-172, 197-202) mutate dataRef in-place before commitData \u2014 config is unaffected because it's rebuilt inside materializeData().",
    "AGENT CHAT PATH (DECISION-004): Agent edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling (useAgentSession.ts:288-295, useTimelineRealtime.ts:27-47), NOT through applyResolvedConfigEdit. These correctly bypass undo (polling commits use skipHistory). Do NOT attempt to make agent edits undoable \u2014 treat them like external/collaborative edits.",
    "DEBT: agent-chat-undo-integration \u2014 agent edits bypass undo stack by design. Do not make this worse by accidentally removing skipHistory from polling paths.",
    "DEBT: conflict-resolution \u2014 silently reloading agent updates can discard dirty local edits. The checkpoint system gives users a recovery path but doesn't fix the root issue. Don't introduce new silent-reload paths.",
    "PRE-MUTATION PROBLEM: Verify asset drop undo works correctly. The snapshot stores config from before the drop; on undo, buildDataFromCurrentRegistry rebuilds from that config, which should remove both the clip AND any auto-created track.",
    "TRANSACTION COLLAPSING: Two mechanisms \u2014 exact transactionId match (drag, resize, multi-clip move) and 300ms time window (untransacted rapid edits like sliders). These are intentionally separate. Don't unify them.",
    "CHECKPOINT EDIT COUNTING (minor flag): Transaction-collapsed gestures still count once per low-level commit toward the 30-edit auto-checkpoint cap. Acceptable for v1 \u2014 a drag of 20 intermediate positions counts as ~20 edits toward checkpoint threshold, not 1. Could refine later.",

  "meta_commentary": "**Execution guidance:**\n\n**Critical path:** T1+T2 (parallel) \u2192 T3+T4 (parallel, T3 needs T1+T2, T4 needs T1) \u2192 T5 (needs T3+T4) \u2192 T6 (needs T5) \u2192 T7+T8+T9+T10+T11+T12 (parallel, various deps). T13 is the integration gate. T14 is deploy.\n\n**The single hardest thing:** T5 (useTimelineHistory) is the bulk of the work. Read `useTimelineSave.ts` thoroughly first \u2014 understand `commitData`, `materializeData`, `applyTimelineEdit`, and `applyResolvedConfigEdit` call flow. The snapshot strategy (config-based, not dataRef-based) is the key design decision. Get this right and everything else follows.\n\n**Pre-mutation problem (most subtle bug risk):** Drop handlers in useAssetManagement and useExternalDrop mutate `dataRef.current.tracks` and `dataRef.current.rows` IN PLACE before calling applyTimelineEdit. The onBeforeCommit callback receives `dataRef.current` which already has these mutations. BUT we only read `.config` and `.signature` from it \u2014 config is rebuilt by `materializeData()` inside `applyTimelineEdit`, so it still reflects the PREVIOUS committed state. This is why we snapshot config, not rows/tracks.\n\n**Agent chat edits (corrected assumption):** The plan originally claimed agent edits participate in undo via applyResolvedConfigEdit. This is WRONG \u2014 agent edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime (useAgentSession.ts:288-295), which triggers polling, which commits with skipHistory. This is actually correct behavior: externally-originated edits should not be undoable. Do not attempt to wire agent edits into the undo system.\n\n**Checkpoint edit counting (known minor imprecision):** During a drag, each intermediate position counts as 1 edit toward the 30-edit checkpoint cap, even though transaction collapsing means they're 1 undo entry. This is acceptable for v1 \u2014 it means checkpoints trigger slightly more often during heavy drag sessions, which is harmless.\n\n**buildDataFromCurrentRegistry:** Verify this function exists in timeline-save-utils.ts before writing T5. It's referenced in the plan but needs confirmation. It should take a TimelineConfig + current TimelineData and rebuild rows/meta/clipOrder/tracks/output from the config while preserving the registry and resolved URLs.\n\n**Deploy order:** Do NOT deploy the migration (T14) until all code changes pass tests (T13). The migration creates the table but existing code won't break without it \u2014 the DataProvider methods are optional.\n\n**Supabase safety:** Deploy with `npx supabase db push --linked` (never `db reset --linked`). Deploy functions individually if any edge functions are touched (none in this plan)."
}

        Idea:
Implement undo/redo/history system for tools/video-editor

## Context
The video editor uses React Context + custom hooks. All mutations flow through applyTimelineEdit() in useTimelineSave.ts. There is no existing undo/redo.

## Requirements

### Two-tier history system:

**Tier 1: Undo Stack (fine-grained, in-memory)**
- Push snapshot of { rows, meta, clipOrder, tracks, output } before every applyTimelineEdit() call
- Cap at ~50-100 entries
- Cmd+Z / Cmd+Shift+Z
- Drag operations and rapid edits should collapse into single undo entries (batch by user gesture using transaction ID or debounce window)

**Tier 2: Checkpoints (coarse-grained, persisted to Supabase)**
- Named snapshots of full TimelineConfig stored in Supabase DB
- Keep checkpoints for past 24 hours
- Browsable - jump to any checkpoint
- Activity-aware checkpoint triggers:
  1. Session boundary: When edit arrives after >5min idle gap, checkpoint pre-edit state
  2. Edit-distance cap: After ~30 edits since last checkpoint, auto-checkpoint
  3. Semantic triggers: Before destructive ops (delete track, bulk delete, clear timeline) + manual Save checkpoint
- Checkpoint metadata: id, timestamp, trigger type, auto-generated label, editsSinceLastCheckpoint, config

### UI
- Undo/redo buttons + history dropdown placed next to the Saving... indicator in the editor chrome
- History panel shows checkpoint list with labels and trigger types

### Persistence
- Checkpoints stored in Supabase (JSONB column or separate table) for past 24 hours
- Undo stack is in-memory only (session-level)

### Implementation approach
- New useTimelineHistory hook that wraps/intercepts applyTimelineEdit
- Exposes: undo(), redo(), jumpToCheckpoint(id), createCheckpoint(label)
- Manages idle timer via ref tracking lastEditTimestamp
- Checkpoints cap at ~20-30, undo stack at ~50-100

    "id": "T5",
    "description": "Create `useTimelineHistory` hook at `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineHistory.ts`. Core implementation: (1) Undo/redo stacks as refs, canUndo/canRedo as state updated on empty\u2194non-empty transitions. (2) `onBeforeCommit` callback snapshots `currentData.config` (NOT dataRef \u2014 avoids pre-mutation problem). (3) Transaction collapsing: skip push if transactionId matches top entry's; 300ms debounce for untransacted rapid edits. (4) Undo: pop from undo stack, push current config to redo, rebuild TimelineData via `buildDataFromCurrentRegistry(snapshot.config, dataRef.current)`, call `commitData(rebuilt, {save:true, skipHistory:true})`. Redo mirrors. (5) Checkpoint triggers inside onBeforeCommit: session boundary (>5min idle), edit-distance (30 edits), semantic flag. Fire-and-forget `provider.saveCheckpoint()`. (6) Load checkpoints on mount. (7) `jumpToCheckpoint`: restore config, clear both stacks. (8) `createManualCheckpoint`: save current config immediately. Cap undo at 100 entries.",
    "depends_on": [
      "T3",
      "T4"
    ],
    "status": "done",

(3) Transaction collapsing: skip push if transactionId matches top entry's; 300ms debounce for untransacted rapid edits. (4) Undo: pop from undo stack, push current config to redo, rebuild TimelineData via `buildDataFromCurrentRegistry(snapshot.config, dataRef.current)`, call `commitData(rebuilt, {save:true, skipHistory:true})`. Redo mirrors. (5) Checkpoint triggers inside onBeforeCommit: session boundary (>5min idle), edit-distance (30 edits), semantic flag. Fire-and-forget `provider.saveCheckpoint()`. (6) Load checkpoints on mount. (7) `jumpToCheckpoint`: restore config, clear both stacks. (8) `createManualCheckpoint`: save current config immediately. Cap undo at 100 entries.",

  "watch_items": [
    "SNAPSHOT CORRECTNESS: Snapshots must capture `currentData.config` (the last committed config), NOT `dataRef.current.rows/tracks`. Drop handlers (useAssetManagement.ts:164-165, useExternalDrop.ts:171-172, 197-202) mutate dataRef in-place before commitData \u2014 config is unaffected because it's rebuilt inside materializeData().",
    "AGENT CHAT PATH (DECISION-004): Agent edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling (useAgentSession.ts:288-295, useTimelineRealtime.ts:27-47), NOT through applyResolvedConfigEdit. These correctly bypass undo (polling commits use skipHistory). Do NOT attempt to make agent edits undoable \u2014 treat them like external/collaborative edits.",
    "DEBT: agent-chat-undo-integration \u2014 agent edits bypass undo stack by design. Do not make this worse by accidentally removing skipHistory from polling paths.",
    "DEBT: conflict-resolution \u2014 silently reloading agent updates can discard dirty local edits. The checkpoint system gives users a recovery path but doesn't fix the root issue. Don't introduce new silent-reload paths.",
    "PRE-MUTATION PROBLEM: Verify asset drop undo works correctly. The snapshot stores config from before the drop; on undo, buildDataFromCurrentRegistry rebuilds from that config, which should remove both the clip AND any auto-created track.",
    "TRANSACTION COLLAPSING: Two mechanisms \u2014 exact transactionId match (drag, resize, multi-clip move) and 300ms time window (untransacted rapid edits like sliders). These are intentionally separate. Don't unify them.",
    "CHECKPOINT EDIT COUNTING (minor flag): Transaction-collapsed gestures still count once per low-level commit toward the 30-edit auto-checkpoint cap. Acceptable for v1 \u2014 a drag of 20 intermediate positions counts as ~20 edits toward checkpoint threshold, not 1. Could refine later.",
    "SKIP_HISTORY COMPLETENESS: reloadFromServer (~line 436) and polled-data sync effect (~line 367) in useTimelineSave.ts must have skipHistory:true. If any new commit path is added during implementation, evaluate whether it should skip history.",
    "buildDataFromCurrentRegistry DEPENDENCY: Confirm this function exists in timeline-save-utils.ts and correctly rebuilds TimelineData from a config + current registry. It must preserve resolved URLs and registry entries.",
    "RLS POLICY: timeline_checkpoints must filter by user_id = auth.uid(). Users should never see other users' checkpoints even if they share a timeline_id.",
    "UNDO STACK SIZE: 100 entries \u00d7 ~10-20KB config = ~1-2MB. Monitor in practice. If configs grow larger (many clips), consider structural sharing or reducing cap.",
    "canUndo/canRedo STATE TRANSITIONS: Only update React state on empty\u2194non-empty transitions to avoid re-rendering the entire editor tree on every edit. Do not use derived state from stack length."
  ],

  "meta_commentary": "**Execution guidance:**\n\n**Critical path:** T1+T2 (parallel) \u2192 T3+T4 (parallel, T3 needs T1+T2, T4 needs T1) \u2192 T5 (needs T3+T4) \u2192 T6 (needs T5) \u2192 T7+T8+T9+T10+T11+T12 (parallel, various deps). T13 is the integration gate. T14 is deploy.\n\n**The single hardest thing:** T5 (useTimelineHistory) is the bulk of the work. Read `useTimelineSave.ts` thoroughly first \u2014 understand `commitData`, `materializeData`, `applyTimelineEdit`, and `applyResolvedConfigEdit` call flow. The snapshot strategy (config-based, not dataRef-based) is the key design decision. Get this right and everything else follows.\n\n**Pre-mutation problem (most subtle bug risk):** Drop handlers in useAssetManagement and useExternalDrop mutate `dataRef.current.tracks` and `dataRef.current.rows` IN PLACE before calling applyTimelineEdit. The onBeforeCommit callback receives `dataRef.current` which already has these mutations. BUT we only read `.config` and `.signature` from it \u2014 config is rebuilt by `materializeData()` inside `applyTimelineEdit`, so it still reflects the PREVIOUS committed state. This is why we snapshot config, not rows/tracks.\n\n**Agent chat edits (corrected assumption):** The plan originally claimed agent edits participate in undo via applyResolvedConfigEdit. This is WRONG \u2014 agent edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime (useAgentSession.ts:288-295), which triggers polling, which commits with skipHistory. This is actually correct behavior: externally-originated edits should not be undoable. Do not attempt to wire agent edits into the undo system.\n\n**Checkpoint edit counting (known minor imprecision):** During a drag, each intermediate position counts as 1 edit toward the 30-edit checkpoint cap, even though transaction collapsing means they're 1 undo entry. This is acceptable for v1 \u2014 it means checkpoints trigger slightly more often during heavy drag sessions, which is harmless.\n\n**buildDataFromCurrentRegistry:** Verify this function exists in timeline-save-utils.ts before writing T5. It's referenced in the plan but needs confirmation. It should take a TimelineConfig + current TimelineData and rebuild rows/meta/clipOrder/tracks/output from the config while preserving the registry and resolved URLs.\n\n**Deploy order:** Do NOT deploy the migration (T14) until all code changes pass tests (T13). The migration creates the table but existing code won't break without it \u2014 the DataProvider methods are optional.\n\n**Supabase safety:** Deploy with `npx supabase db push --linked` (never `db reset --linked`). Deploy functions individually if any edge functions are touched (none in this plan)."
}

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-panel-tabs: text clip multi-selections would miss the position tab under the plan's stated rule. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] bulk-properties-panel: bulkclippanel still imports customeffecteditor; removing it without updating bulk mode would break the build. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] clip-drag-scroll-sync: the plan's step 3 ontick callback only shows coordinator.update() but the full clip-drag state path (snap, lateststart, ghost positions) also needs re-running during stationary-pointer scroll. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] conflict-resolution: silently reloading agent-originated timeline updates would discard dirty local edits. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] context-menu-scope: multi-clip copy is not included despite being mentioned in the original idea. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] issue-detection: mid-sentence-cut detection is not implementable without transcript/profile data that the current provider doesn't expose. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] legacy-effect-lifecycle: removing customeffecteditor makes effects-table entries read-only — users can no longer edit legacy custom effects. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] optimistic-locking: plan only specifies version checking at the db write layer; the ui load/state/save path doesn't carry config_version yet. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] properties-panel-scope: crop and transition controls are not included in the bulk panel, narrowing the original idea's scope. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] realtime-subscriptions: the shared realtime system doesn't know about timeline_agent_sessions; step 7 hooks need their own channel subscription. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] selection-save-bridge: the plan specifies passing pruneselection into usetimelinesave but doesn't detail the hook-layer bridge needed because usetimelinesave is instantiated inside usetimelinedata before usetimelinestate runs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
